1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the combinatorial space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 207 and the convergence rate is O(106/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the combinatorial space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 207 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 106. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on combinatorial structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 880. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 762 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to combinatorial theory, we decompose the problem into 880 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 762. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
